- Migration `031`: `listing_settings_groups.content` JSONB default `{ stripBrandFromTitle, aiTitleEnabled, aiDescriptionEnabled }` all false.
- UI: Settings → Listing Group drawer general step (toggles + hints). Shared: `ListingContentConfig`, Zod `listingContentConfigSchema`.
- Apply strip-brand + optional AI in `ListingStrategyService` at **create** only — see "Listing content policy" above.

### Auth / session security (implemented)
- **Refresh token**: HttpOnly cookie `sellerhill_rt` (`path=/api`, `SameSite=Lax`, `Secure` in production). JS cannot read it.
- **Access token**: memory only (Redux) — never `localStorage` / `sessionStorage`.
- **Boot**: `AuthBootstrap` calls `POST /auth/refresh` with `credentials: 'include'`; on success hydrates access token.
- **401**: `baseApi` single-flight cookie refresh, then retry; failure → logout.
- **Logout**: `POST /auth/logout` clears cookie + Redux wipe. Legacy `localStorage` token keys are purged on load.
- Do **not** reintroduce `localStorage.setItem('accessToken'|'refreshToken')`.
- **CORS**: resolved in `main.ts` (see "Secrets / env checklist"). Production must list the real web origin(s).
- **Cookie env**: optional `COOKIE_DOMAIN` (e.g. `.takci.cloud`), `COOKIE_SAMESITE=lax|none`.
- **Forgot / reset password**: `POST /auth/forgot-password` (`@Throttle` 3/min/IP) → always the same generic `200 { message: 'auth.passwordReset.emailSent' }`; it sends a link **only** for an `active` account with a non-null `password_hash` (Google-only / pending / inactive / banned get nothing, and the response never varies — no enumeration). A per-account 60s cooldown (`AUTH_CONSTANTS.PASSWORD_RESET_COOLDOWN_SECONDS`) suppresses a second mail while the first link is live. The link carries a 256-bit opaque token; only its SHA-256 is stored in `password_reset_tokens` (migration `092`), single-use, 60-min TTL (`PASSWORD_RESET_TOKEN_TTL_MINUTES`). `POST /auth/reset-password { token, password }` verifies the hash, rejects a password equal to the current one (`auth.errors.samePassword`), then in one transaction sets the new hash, bumps `session_version`, revokes every `auth_refresh_sessions` row, and consumes the token **plus all siblings** — so a completed reset kills every existing session and issues **no** new one (the client is sent to `/login`). Any invalid/expired/used token → `401 auth.errors.resetTokenInvalid` (one message for all cases). Pure bits in `password-reset-helpers.ts` (Jest-covered); the `password_reset` email template rows (en+tr) are seeded in `092`. FE: `apps/web/src/features/auth/{forgot-password,reset-password}/` (strict 4-file split, shared `AuthShowcase` panel), a "Forgot password?" link on `LoginPage`, and locale-less `App.tsx` redirects — `reset-password` with `preserveQuery` to carry the token. Design spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-02-forgot-password-design.md`.
- **Google OAuth (GIS popup auth-code)**: optional. Env: `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` + `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` (api), `VITE_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` (web — same Client ID). FE `@react-oauth/google` popup yields a one-time `code` → `POST /auth/google` → backend `google-auth-library` exchanges it (`redirectUri: 'postmessage'`) + `verifyIdToken`, then issues a normal session. **Never auto-merges** a Google identity into an existing password account — same email with a password account → `409 emailExistsPassword`. Google skips only name entry + email verification; onboarding/eBay connect/routing are identical to password login (same `{accessToken,user}` + `sellerhill_rt` cookie via `attachSession`). Pure `google-link-decision.ts` (login/create/block) is unit-tested. Google-only users have `password_hash = NULL`; password login is blocked for them (`invalidCredentials`). Missing env → API boots, endpoint returns `503 googleNotConfigured`, FE hides the button. Schema: migration `039`. Design spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-google-oauth-design.md`.

### Domain UI vs design system
- Product-specific composites live in `apps/web/src/domain-ui/` (`ListingCard`, `ConnectEbayPrompt`, `ProductTableCell`).
- `@repo/ui` is design-system only (atoms/molecules/organisms + tokens). Do not re-add domain widgets there.
- **`ProductTableCell` is the product identity cell for EVERY table** (listings, orders, products) — three drifted hand-rolled copies were folded into it. Props are `title` (always fully on the tooltip), `imageUrl`, `meta` (labelled `IdBadge` rows) and a `subtitle` slot. A new table showing a product uses this — do not write another.

### Route metadata
- Breadcrumbs: `apps/web/src/app/routeMeta.ts` (`APP_ROUTE_META`, `resolveRouteMeta`, `resolveBreadcrumbs`). It describes the seller app only.
- Do not hardcode path→label maps in `AppLayout`.

### Settings surface
- Canonical UI: `/settings` hub + drawers only.
- Legacy full pages (`/settings/store`, `/settings/amazon-accounts`, `/settings/listing-groups`) redirect to the hub; `/settings/billing` redirects to `/billing`.
- **Gated by `EbayAccountGuard`** — most hub sections (Store Settings, Listing Settings Groups, buyer-messaging templates) only make sense once an eBay store exists.

### eBay connection gate (`EbayAccountGuard` + `ConnectEbayPrompt`)
Every page whose data is meaningless without a connected store wraps its container's return in `<EbayAccountGuard>` (`apps/web/src/components/EbayAccountGuard.tsx`): it queries `useGetEbayAccountsQuery()` itself and swaps `children` for `ConnectEbayPrompt` when the account list is empty. It is opted into per-container, not wired into the route table or `AppLayout` — a new data page must explicitly add it. Current usages: Action Center, Dashboard, Listings (all/overview/jobs/settings-groups), Products, Orders, Settings.
- **`ConnectEbayPrompt`** (`apps/web/src/domain-ui/ConnectEbayPrompt/`) is the ONE "connect your eBay store" visual — built on `EmptyState` inside a `styled(Card)` capped at a centered `32rem`, used by the guard fallback, the `/onboarding/ebay` page and `StoresPage`'s empty state. Do not hand-roll another. `footnote` is the only content that varies (the onboarding page's US-only marketplace note); `onSkip` is optional and only supplied where skipping makes sense (onboarding, the guard's dashboard fallback) — never on `StoresPage`.
- `EmptyState` has `isActionLoading?: boolean` (forwarded to the primary `Button`'s `isLoading`) — any empty state whose action is an async call uses it instead of re-implementing the button.

### One canonical flow per task (no duplicate surfaces)
Same rule as "one admin panel, no duplicates" and the settings-hub redirects:
- **Add listings** is the `AddListingsDrawer` only. `/listings/add` redirects to `/listings?drawer=add`; do not reintroduce a standalone page (a second implementation over the same schema silently drifts).
- **`/orders` IS the orders list — there is no orders overview.** `/orders/all` redirects to `/orders` preserving the query string. Orders have no create action (they arrive from eBay), so an overview would be a third copy of `OrderCarousel` (the dashboard Cards tab already renders it). Do not reintroduce it.
- Before adding a page that duplicates an existing flow, add a tab, a drawer, or a query param instead.

### Tabs — one rail, one meaning
- **`TabNav`** (`packages/ui/src/atoms/TabNav/`) is the only tab rail: controlled (`items` / `value` / `onChange`), navigation-only, scrolls instead of wrapping — **except below `breakpoints.sm`, where it WRAPS onto a second row** (operator, 2026-10-01: "on mobile the tabs do not fit the screen"; a scrolled-off tab with a hidden scrollbar is an invisible one, and on a phone there is no right-hand filter the rail has to stay level with). `Tabs` composes it when you also want it to own the panel content.
- Page-level section navigation (Dashboard, Action Center) = `TabNav` `underline`. The admin console's sections are operator-sidebar entries on one route (`?tab=`, see `operatorRouting.ts`), not a tab rail. Compact in-card switches (chart granularity, billing interval) = `SegmentedControl`. Grid↔table = `ViewToggle`.
- **Never use `Button variant="primary|secondary"` as a tab or filter group** — it makes "where you already are" the loudest element on the page and leaves no weight for real CTAs. For the same reason, per-row Save buttons in a settings list are `secondary` — a tab full of primaries means nothing is primary.

### No hand-rolled primitives (the recurring failure mode)
Every one of these existed as a private copy before being folded back into the design system. Reach for the atom first; if it can't express what you need, add a variant to the atom rather than forking it.
- **Cards** → `styled(Card)`.
- **Textareas** → `Textarea`, which carries `fill` (absolute-inset, fills a positioned card) and `mono` (code/HTML).
- **Buttons** → `Button` / `IconButton`. Carousel arrows, pagination dots and "view all" links must not be raw `styled.button`s. A pagination dot needs a ≥1.5rem hit area with a small visual mark inside — never make the 0.5rem dot itself the button.
- **Empty / error screens** → `EmptyState`; **a list's first load → the `DataTable` skeleton** (`loading` prop), never an `EmptyState` spinner card, which collapses the page height and makes everything around it jump. A non-list surface keeps `EmptyState` for loading, the same component as its empty state. The `ErrorBoundary` fallback uses it too.
- **Nested flows** → a real nested `Drawer` with `onBack`, not inline content swapped into the parent's card.

### Page-level loading is not the global overlay
`useLoading(...)` takes **mutation flags only**. Folding an initial `useQuery` `isLoading` into it covers the whole app with the blocking overlay on first paint. Initial fetch of a LIST renders the `DataTable`'s skeleton (`loading`, card-shaped placeholders — a title line, a thumbnail with text lines, a stat footer — so the grid keeps its height when the real cards arrive; jobs, products, revision history, listings, drafts, orders, returns, Best Sellers); any other page renders its own `EmptyState` (loading title + description). Never the overlay.
**The blocking overlay is SellerHill's own, and scrims are dimmed, never blurred (2026-10-02, operator request).** `S.LoadingOverlay` (shared shell, both layouts) is a `colors.surface.loadingOverlay` scrim (black 35%) over the unchanged page — no white wash, no `backdrop-filter` — with a navy `S.LoadingCard` holding `<Logo layout="wordmark" />` (white SELLER needs a dark surface) and an amber indeterminate bar (`prefers-reduced-motion` freezes it). `Modal` and `Drawer` overlays use `colors.surface.overlay` (black 40%) with no `backdrop-filter`; do not put a blur back on a scrim — the page behind must stay legible. (Frosted glass panes and the sticky header keep their own blur.)

### Listings CSV export
- `GET /listings/export` — same filters as list (incl. store/status/ranges), server-built CSV, max 5000 rows.
- FE: `useExportListingsCsvMutation` (not client-side CSV from a page of items).

Persistence is what makes a retried eBay push stop re-buying a paid tracking number — see "Tracking conversion (Aquiline)" |

Prices unchanged. AO is derived (`= conversions × 2`), never multiplied in place — an in-place multiply is not idempotent. **The 2× rule is superseded by `125_billing_prices_v3_unlimited_ao_best_sellers.sql` (AO unlimited).** |

See "Tracking conversion (Aquiline)". |

| `092` | `password_reset_tokens` (hashed single-use reset tokens, `user_id` FK cascade, unique `token_hash`, partial index on unconsumed rows) + seeds the `password_reset` email template (en+tr). See "Forgot / reset password". |

Keeps `user_role`/`auth_refresh_sessions` (auth), `llm_model_pricing` + the live `llm_usage_log` cost columns, and `usage_events`/`shared_cost_entries` (admin Costs). See "Customer support widget — tawk.to". |

Precondition: the Aquiline API key must be set at `/admin` → Settings, or every shipment is HELD rather than shipped with the raw number. |

See "eBay Picture Services". |
| `120` | Drops the R2 mirror's columns and `116`'s index, superseded by `119`. `116`–`118` are not edited; an applied migration is never amended. |
| `121` | `ebay_rate_limits` — a single row holding the last `getRateLimits` response (`resources` JSONB, `fetched_at`). Deletes the hand-typed `ebay.budget.*DailyLimit` overrides — ceilings come from eBay alone. See "eBay API call budget & bulk writes". |
| `122` | Replaces every catalog template's `sample_data` (by slug) with unbranded products whose photos are served same-origin from `/template-samples/`. The landing preview script and the guard spec read the same JSON literal. |
| `123` | Tech Gadgets template: `.sh-tg-image img` capped at `min(100%, 500px)` so the image no longer overflows narrow containers such as the settings-drawer preview. |
| `124` | `products.stock_status VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'exact'` (`SourceStockStatus`), `products.max_order_quantity INT NULL` (caps the listed quantity), `products.source_removed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL` (+ partial index — a real 404; cleared when observed again and on a Keepa rollback). See "Scraper provider". |
| `125` | **Two migrations carry the number 125** (they were written on parallel branches); the runner applies and records them by FILE NAME in lexical order (`migration-runner.ts` `.sort()`), so both run, `125_billing…` before `125_ebay…`. Neither is renumbered — an applied migration is never renamed. **`125_billing_prices_v3_unlimited_ao_best_sellers.sql`** — billing v3 (2026-09-29): closes every `083` monthly price row (`effective_to`) and inserts the v3 prices (Lite $24.99 … Enterprise $339.99, Starter unchanged — existing subscribers move from their next renewal via `PriceMigrationProcessor`, no operator step); sets `amazon_orders_per_month` to `-1` (unlimited) on every plan and the trial; adds `best_sellers_products_per_month` per plan (Trial 500 … Enterprise 75,000); seeds the three `best-sellers-*` top-up packs; creates `best_sellers_views` (UNIQUE `(user_id, view_key, viewed_on)`), which the allowance is counted from; raises `bestSellers.dailyFetchLimit`'s default to 1000 as a hidden brake. The addon Stripe Product name/description is now chosen per dimension (`describeAddonProduct`, `stripe-catalog-sync.ts`) — it was hardcoded to "extra tracking conversions". See "Customer packages", "The priced meter", "Amazon Best Sellers browsing". |
| `125` | **`125_ebay_messages.sql`** — `ebay_accounts.granted_scopes` (TEXT[], default `{}`) + `unread_message_count`/`unread_message_synced_at` + `message_subscription_id`/`message_subscription_at`; `ebay_notification_destinations` (one row per environment/endpoint), append-only `ebay_notification_events` and `ebay_notification_raw_captures`. See "eBay Messages". |
| `128` | Order change tracking + returns: `orders.ebay_cancel_state` / `ebay_cancelled_at` / `ebay_refunded_amount` / `ebay_refunded_at` (+ partial index on cancelled orders), the `ebay_returns` table (UNIQUE `(ebay_account_id, return_id)`; one row per eBay return, overwritten by each sweep) and `ebay_accounts.last_return_sync_at` (+ partial index on active stores). See "Order change tracking" and "eBay Returns". (`126`/`127` are template-catalog migrations.) |
| `130` | `orders.buyer_data_erased_at` + `ebay_returns.buyer_data_erased_at` — the durable marker of an eBay account-deletion erasure, so a later re-read never restores the buyer's data; past erasures are back-marked from `audit_logs`. See "eBay Marketplace Account Deletion notification". |
| `131` | `orders.ebay_line_item_id VARCHAR(50)` — the eBay ORDER LINE ITEM id a shipping fulfillment must name (not the listing id). Written at order sync, or by one `getOrder` read at push time for older orders. See "eBay Fulfillment API". |
| `132` | Order flow hardening: `orders.auto_fulfill_submitted_at` (the Place Order click stamp; existing `no_confirmation` / `interrupted` blocks and any row still RUNNING at boot are stamped so they read as "purchase not confirmed"), `orders.auto_fulfill_suspect_amazon_order_id`, `orders.ebay_line_item_count`, `orders.ebay_ship_by_date`, `store_settings.auto_fulfill_max_loss`, and the append-only `auto_fulfill_events` audit table. See "Automated Amazon Fulfillment (A2)". |
| `133` | `orders.delivered_at` (the tracking tick that read "Delivered"; shown by the order timeline), `auto_fulfill_status` DEFAULT `pending` → `skipped`, and a one-time fix of rows stuck at `pending` with no job behind them (untracked, or over a day old and untouched for an hour, never one with a click stamp). See "Order Sync" and "Order detail". |
| `134` | `listing_revisions.previous_source_stock` / `previous_source_stock_status` / `new_source_stock` / `new_source_stock_status` — the Amazon stock at each revision beside the eBay quantity. See "Listing detail". |
| `135` | Buyer-message defaults, second wording: greeting by `{{buyer_name}}`, the shipped message carries the tracking number + carrier pushed to eBay. Rewrites `buyer_message_system_defaults`, updates sellers' unedited starter templates, replaces `{{buyer_username}}` in the rest. See "Buyer Auto-Messaging". |
| `137` | Adds the `mini` plan below Lite: $19.99/month, 100 listings, 20 tracking conversions, 1,000 Best Sellers products, unlimited automatic orders; `display_order` 5. All four limit rows are written explicitly (an absent row resolves to unlimited). The Stripe Product/Price is minted by the hourly catalog sync. See "Customer packages". |
| `138` | Listing rules + VeRO + clean-up + scheduled listing: `store_settings.listing_rules` JSONB, the platform `vero_keywords` table (unique on `LOWER(keyword)`), `listings.quantity_zero_since` (kept by the `listings_track_quantity_zero` trigger; existing zero-quantity rows start their clock at migration time), `listings.auto_ended_reason` / `auto_ended_at` / `auto_end_failed_at`, `listing_jobs.scheduled_until`, and for Promoted Listings `ebay_accounts.promoted_campaign_id` + `listings.promoted_ad_rate`. See "Listing rules, VeRO protection, clean-up and scheduled listing". |
| `139` | `ds-general-store` gets a horizontal inset (`.sh-gs-main` / `.sh-gs-details` 24px, footer margin, 16px under 600px; spec rows lose their own side padding to line up with their heading — text sat flush against the frame) and every template's `sample_data` loses its `Brand:` / `Manufacturer:` rows (`has_details` recomputed). Patched with `replace()` per rule, which `build-template-previews.mjs` parses. See "A description never shows the brand". |
| `140` | `store_settings.allow_cross_store_asins BOOLEAN NULL` — may a store list an ASIN already on the seller's other stores (NULL = inherit global; global NULL = off). See "ASIN intake". |
| `141` | Listing rules move into each settings group: `listing_settings_groups.listing_rules` JSONB (NULL = defaults) and `store_settings.blocked_asins` JSONB (NULL = inherit). Copies each user's global `listing_rules` (minus `blockedAsins` / `promotedAdRate`) into every group and each row's blocked list into its own column; drops nothing. See "Listing rules, VeRO protection, clean-up and scheduled listing". |
| `136` | `orders.seller_note TEXT` — the seller's own private note on an order. Never written by order sync; cleared by the eBay account-deletion erasure. See "Order stages". |
| `129` | One-time watermark rewind (`last_ebay_sync_at = GREATEST(created_at, NOW() - 90 days)`) so the first order-sync tick after deploy re-reads recently modified orders and fills their cancel/refund state. Its own file because `128` had already been applied somewhere when the statement was written — an applied migration never re-runs. |

API runs pending migrations on boot (`DatabaseService.onModuleInit` → `MigrationRunner`). Production Docker also runs `migrate` in entrypoint. **Restart API** after pulling new SQL files.

**The schema must apply to a STOCK Postgres — no extension beyond `uuid-ossp`/`pgcrypto` may be mandatory.** Local dev's `docker-compose.yml` pins `pgvector/pgvector:0.8.1-pg16`, so a pgvector dependency is invisible there, but Coolify/managed Postgres is plain `postgres:*-alpine`. An unconditional `CREATE EXTENSION vector` in `046` once aborted the entire boot-time migration run and crash-looped the API before it could listen ("deploy succeeded but the container is Exited"). `046` is now guarded (checks `pg_available_extensions` and skips its vector objects), and its tables were dropped by `093` anyway; if the assistant is ever revived, point the database at a pgvector image and ship the tables as a **new** migration.
- **Verify a schema change against a stock image, not just local dev**: `docker run -d --name t -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=x -e POSTGRES_DB=testdb postgres:18-alpine`, `CREATE ROLE sellerhill_user LOGIN;` (migration `021` grants to it), then pipe every file in order through `psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1`. That reproduces the deployment target in about a minute.

### Package builds agents must remember
- `@repo/shared` and **`@repo/ui` load from `dist/`** — after editing icons, Table selection width, Checkbox, etc., run `pnpm --filter @repo/ui build` (and shared when types/i18n change) or the `pnpm dev` package build step.
- Icon names must exist in `packages/ui/src/atoms/Icon/icons/index.tsx` (e.g. `sliders-horizontal` alias). Emotion **must not** use component selectors like `${Other}:hover &` without babel plugin (crashes at runtime).

### Deferred (optional / env-dependent)
- CSRF token layer if API is ever cross-site with `SameSite=None`.
- Bulk offline AI rewrite of existing 100k listings (not in online create path).
- Push title/policy edits from listing detail to eBay Inventory API (`updateListing` writes the DB only today; price/qty still driven by refresh + strategy).

## Frontend Rules (Quick Reference)

See `.claude/skills/frontend-rules/SKILL.md` for the canonical version.

### File organization
4 files per feature component: `.component.tsx` (markup only) / `.container.tsx` (logic) / `.style.ts` (styled) / `.types.ts` (types). Stateful atoms/molecules (Select, Dropdown, etc.) also need `.container.tsx` + `.component.tsx` split. Stateless atoms (Button, Badge) stay as `.component.tsx` + `.style.ts` + `.types.ts`. Exempt: landing, RTK api files, store.ts, configs.

Heavy page logic belongs in `features/<x>/hooks/` — not a 600-line container.

### Anti-patterns (will be blocked by hook + lint)
- `styled(...)` outside `.style.ts`
- `useState`/`useEffect`/RTK Query/etc. in `.component.tsx`
- `interface`/`type`/`enum` outside `.types.ts`
- Hardcoded hex/rgb colors (use `tkn('colors.*')`)
- Hardcoded px/rem spacing (use `tkn('spacing.*')`)
- `style={{ }}` inline styles
- `styled.h1`/`styled.p` (use `<Text variant="...">`)
- Raw `<select>`, `<input>`, `<button>`, native HTML form controls — and `styled.button` / `styled.select` in a feature `.style.ts` (use `Button`, `IconButton`, `Select`)
- External labels above TextInput/Select (use floating `label` prop)
- Mismatched control heights or non-brand focus rings on form controls
- `alert()`/`confirm()` (use `MessageModal` via `showMessage`)
- Hardcoded status strings like `'active'` (use enums from `packages/shared`)
- Hardcoded UI strings (use i18n `t()`, with the key added to every locale folder — `admin`/`legal` are en + tr only)
- Two families competing for the **same** role (e.g. body text Inter on one screen, Lexend on another). A heading/body pairing is not this — the sanctioned stack is Inter headings + Lexend body/UI + JetBrains Mono for codes. Never hardcode a family name; use the tokens.
- Hardcoded `@media` breakpoint literals or raw `window.innerWidth` checks (use `tkn('breakpoints.*')` / an exported px constant) — see Architectural Rule 13, Mobile-Web Responsiveness.
- Disabling Submit/Continue on incomplete required fields instead of validating on click — see "Required-field validation" in `.claude/skills/frontend-rules/SKILL.md`: track a `submitAttempted` flag, flag empty fields red via the field atom's own `errorMessage`/`error` prop on the failed attempt, never a static "N fields required" info box.

### Atom extension pattern in `.style.ts`
Empty template literal + variant/weight/size props in JSX. Layout CSS only in template (margin/gap/flex/grid/position/dimensions). No font-size/font-weight/color/background/border/shadow in template — those go in props.
